## 2026-11-03 KST — G238 owner-selected LOCAL typed CSV with approval-bound preview, aggregation, create-only export/audit (5/5 CI SUCCESS)

Operator chooses and approves actual file, not AI; do not run on customer files in CI.
- **Design comparison:** (A) arbitrary user data into Docker build context risks persisting source bytes in Docker images; (B) native NAS execution still lacks registered Expert contract/permission; (C) strict stdlib local file reader, four code-owned typed operators, raw input never in public logs, no external network/CLI/process and independent export consent = selected. No Python eval/shell, Docker mount, new external service, or owner-file edits. File size 32KiB, <=256 rows, <=32 groups, one-hop CSV, names limited to safe ASCII labels and decimal cents; prevents formula injection, strange paths, traversal, link inputs and overwrite. SHA receipt is local integrity only, not notarization. Windows link race guarantees remain limited by platform APIs.

## 2026-11-03 KST — G235 real disconnected typed DAG Docker execution (5/5 exact CI SUCCESS)

- **Product capability:** G234 proven Docker sandbox ran only one file-inventory fixed fixture; G235 now builds a four-step code-owned typed DAG `csv_read → validate_decimal → group_sum → report` from two exact synthetic ASCII files, compiles one static C checker, and executes it in a locally digest-addressed `FROM scratch` image. ## G149 CI_VERIFIED — 2026-11-01 KST — official Checker Docker transport limited to LOCAL daemon; no remote owner-file mounts
- **Material security issue fixed:** Existing tools/flowpilot3_official_checker.py Docker mode ran `docker image inspect` with inherited caller DOCKER_HOST/CONTEXT/TLS and default configuration, and `docker run` did not explicitly set `--pull=never`. A configured remote Docker daemon could receive the owner-private G103/G139/G147 candidate as a bind mount. Now fail closed unless a non-symlink, local Unix /var/run/docker.sock is present; force `--host=unix:///var/run/docker.sock` for inspect AND run; create one temporary empty Docker config, pass sanitized PATH/HOME/DOCKER_CONFIG/DOCKER_HOST only; never inherit DOCKER_CONTEXT/TLS/certificate settings or local credentials; ensure image preinstalled and run `--pull=never`, `--network=none`, read-only root, nonroot user, cap-drop, no-new-privileges, PID/memory/CPU limits, no host networking or user selected mounts.
